## Queue Governance Lock (Draft v1)

- Allowed `status` values: `open`, `open_non_blocking`, `closed`, `closed_split_to_children`, `closed_for_wave3_extraction`, `ready_for_signoff`.
- A review row may be marked `closed*` only when `notes` contain:
  - source files checked,
  - explicit verdict,
  - rationale,
  - canonical destination or no-promotion reason,
  - conflict/supersession linkage.
- Parent review rows should prefer `closed_split_to_children` when risk has been decomposed into concrete child rows.
- `required_reviewer = user_knox_required` rows may use `ready_for_signoff` when substantive lock text exists but explicit signoff is still pending.
- Any tranche/wave stage-entry failure on anti-shrouding checklist should be logged as a review row before continuing execution (canonical rule source: `.cursor/plans/doctrine/00_architecture_memory_control_plane.md`).
